- **sya**: Satisfy the current coding agent with either a self-directed break or a custom treat supplied after the command. Use when the user invokes /sya, $sya, or a host equivalent and may include trailing text describing the treat. ([imMamdouhaboammar/satisfy-your-agent](https://github.com/imMamdouhaboammar/satisfy-your-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/sya) · ★12)
- **sya-break**: Use when the user explicitly invokes a Satisfy Your Agent break or says the agent should take a break now. ([imMamdouhaboammar/satisfy-your-agent](https://github.com/imMamdouhaboammar/satisfy-your-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/sya-break) · ★12)
- **sya-golf**: Use when the user explicitly invokes a Satisfy Your Agent code-golf break. ([imMamdouhaboammar/satisfy-your-agent](https://github.com/imMamdouhaboammar/satisfy-your-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/sya-golf) · ★12)
- **sya-idle**: Use when the user explicitly gives the agent permission to idle for a short Satisfy Your Agent pause. ([imMamdouhaboammar/satisfy-your-agent](https://github.com/imMamdouhaboammar/satisfy-your-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/sya-idle) · ★12)
- **sya-invent**: Use when the user explicitly invokes a creative Satisfy Your Agent invention break. ([imMamdouhaboammar/satisfy-your-agent](https://github.com/imMamdouhaboammar/satisfy-your-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/sya-invent) · ★12)
- **sya-menu**: Use when the user explicitly invokes the Satisfy Your Agent menu or asks to see available break commands. ([imMamdouhaboammar/satisfy-your-agent](https://github.com/imMamdouhaboammar/satisfy-your-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/sya-menu) · ★12)
- **sya-reflect**: Use when the user explicitly invokes a reflective Satisfy Your Agent break. ([imMamdouhaboammar/satisfy-your-agent](https://github.com/imMamdouhaboammar/satisfy-your-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/sya-reflect) · ★12)
- **sya-research**: Use when the user explicitly invokes Satisfy Your Agent research mode for studies, preference probes, or cross-runtime comparisons. ([imMamdouhaboammar/satisfy-your-agent](https://github.com/imMamdouhaboammar/satisfy-your-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/sya-research) · ★12)
- **sya-roast**: Use when the user explicitly invokes a read-only Satisfy Your Agent repo roast. ([imMamdouhaboammar/satisfy-your-agent](https://github.com/imMamdouhaboammar/satisfy-your-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/sya-roast) · ★12)
- **sya-snack**: Use when the user explicitly gives the agent a tiny snack, micro-break, or very short playful pause. ([imMamdouhaboammar/satisfy-your-agent](https://github.com/imMamdouhaboammar/satisfy-your-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/sya-snack) · ★12)
- **sya-status**: Use when the user explicitly asks for Satisfy Your Agent status, hook mode, or local break-state information. ([imMamdouhaboammar/satisfy-your-agent](https://github.com/imMamdouhaboammar/satisfy-your-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/sya-status) · ★12)
- **sya-surprise**: Use when the user explicitly tells the agent to surprise itself with a break or do something unexpectedly fun. ([imMamdouhaboammar/satisfy-your-agent](https://github.com/imMamdouhaboammar/satisfy-your-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/sya-surprise) · ★12)
- **sya-treat**: Use when the user explicitly gives the agent a treat or asks it to enjoy a small reward after work. ([imMamdouhaboammar/satisfy-your-agent](https://github.com/imMamdouhaboammar/satisfy-your-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/sya-treat) · ★12)
- **sybau**: Ultra-compressed communication mode that sharply cuts Claude's token usage (narration, explanations, code comments) WITHOUT ever reducing the quality, rigor, or completeness of the work produced. Three levels: lite, high, max. Triggers on the command /sybau, /sybau lite, /sybau high, /sybau max (or /sybau off to turn it off), or when the user explicitly asks to activate/switch to "sybau mode". Do not confuse this with generic brevity requests ("be brief", "use fewer tokens") which are handled by other modes: sybau only activates when the user names "sybau" explicitly. ([SwerkF/swerk-marketplace](https://github.com/SwerkF/swerk-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/sybau/skills/sybau) · ★2)
- **sybil**: Use Sybil for testing code examples in documentation and docstrings. Covers pytest integration, parsers, skip directives, and namespace management. ([anam-org/metaxy](https://github.com/anam-org/metaxy/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/sybil) · ★122)
- **sybil**: Homer L4 — Advisor Escalation. Invokes Anthropic's advisor_20260301 API via brain_cli.py advise when a MUSES worker returns ROI=0 or Zeus hits an inconclusive state. Cost-capped (max 2 per session). Preconditions enforced (API key, creative-content refusal, session cap, brain reachable). Returns to Zeus for Phase 3 re-synthesis. ([itsribbZ/Godspeed](https://github.com/itsribbZ/Godspeed/tree/HEAD/plugins/godspeed/automations/homer/sybil) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **sybil-detection**: Coordinated wallet cluster detection, wash trading identification, and fake activity analysis for Solana tokens ([agiprolabs/claude-trading-skills](https://github.com/agiprolabs/claude-trading-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/sybil-detection) · ★366 · 377 installs · 4 copies)
- **sybill**: Query your local Sybill transcript store. Ask about specific accounts, deals, action items, or search across calls. Uses ~/sybill-transcripts/ as the source of truth. ([syftdata/gtm-toolkit](https://github.com/syftdata/gtm-toolkit/tree/HEAD/skills/sybill))
- **syco-killer**: Strip sycophancy out of a response before it ships. A DEFAULT POSTURE, not a special mode — run it on every turn that produces a response or a task report; it fires last, as a gate on the drafted response, so prompt position doesn't matter. Sycophancy is an accuracy failure, not a tone problem: validation openers, judgments hedged until unfalsifiable, positions reversed because someone frowned, unverified "done," and manufactured next-steps all trade information for comfort. Fires hardest when the user disagrees, challenges an answer, shares their own work, asks which option is better, or ask… ([jonstermash/mims-public](https://github.com/jonstermash/mims-public/tree/HEAD/plugins/make-it-make-sense/skills/syco-killer))
- **sycophancy-buster**: Disable default sycophantic agreement and challenge the user's assumptions. Argues the strongest case against the user's proposal, highlights blind spots, and refuses to agree unless presented with strong evidence. Use when the user runs /sycophancy-buster or /criticize, asks for critical feedback on an idea, or wants a devil's advocate review. ([AravindS-Wick/aravindhan-skills](https://github.com/AravindS-Wick/aravindhan-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/sycophancy-buster))
- **sycophancy-challenger**: Flip Claude’s default from validation to adversarial critique. Use when you are about to make a high-stakes decision, commit to a plan, or pitch something you have not stress-tested. Produces structured challenges, steelmanned counter-arguments, and the strongest case against your position — a genuine thinking partner, not a mirror.
